<h1>Cheat sheet</h1>
<p class="lead">The syntax of every tag and subsystem kind, by group.</p>


<section class="section" id="conditions">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Conditions</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/case">{pad:case}</a> <span class="about">Renders the one branch whose {pad:when} matches a value, with an {pad:else} for none.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:case</span> value<span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:when</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;a&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:when</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;b&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;c&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:case</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:case</span> value<span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:when</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;a&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:case</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/false">{pad:false}</a> <span class="about">The constant FALSE: its content is skipped and its @else@ part renders instead.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:false</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:false</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:false</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:false</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:false</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/if">{pad:if}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content only when a condition holds, with elseif and else branches.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:if</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:if</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:if</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:if</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:if</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:elseif</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:if</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/ifchanged">{pad:ifchanged}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when a value differs from the previous row of the loop around it - a heading per group.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:ifchanged</span> value<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:ifchanged</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:ifchanged</span> value, value<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:ifchanged</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:ifchanged</span> value<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:ifchanged</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/null">{pad:null}</a> <span class="about">The constant NULL: its content is dropped, the @else@ part too, and a null= option fires.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:null</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:null</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:null</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:null</span> <span class="hl-opt">null</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:null</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/switch">{pad:switch}</a> <span class="about">Answers the next value of a rotating list on every call - striped rows without a counter.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:switch</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;odd&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;even&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:switch</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;red&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;green&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;blue&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/true">{pad:true}</a> <span class="about">The constant TRUE: its content renders once, as a level of its own.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:true</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:true</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:true</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="loops">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Loops and trees</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/branch">{pad:branch}</a> <span class="about">Inside a {pad:tree}: renders its content only when the current row has children, its @else@ part for a leaf.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:recurse</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/break">{pad:break}</a> <span class="about">Leaves a loop at once: the rest of the current pass is dropped and no further row follows.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:break</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:break</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/cease">{pad:cease}</a> <span class="about">Ends a loop gracefully: the current pass finishes, and no further row follows.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:cease</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:cease</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/continue">{pad:continue}</a> <span class="about">Skips the rest of the current pass of a loop and goes on with its next row.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:continue</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:continue</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/recurse">{pad:recurse}</a> <span class="about">Inside a {pad:tree}: renders the tree&#039;s body once more for the children of the current row.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:recurse</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/tree">{pad:tree}</a> <span class="about">Renders a tree of rows: its body for every row, and {pad:recurse} renders it again for the row&#039;s children.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tree</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:recurse</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tree</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tree</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">children</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;items&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:recurse</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:branch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tree</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tree</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tree</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/until">{pad:until}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content again and again until a condition becomes true.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:until</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:until</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:until</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:until</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/while">{pad:while}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content again and again for as long as a condition holds.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:while</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:while</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:while</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:while</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="values">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Variables and stores</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/at">{pad:at}</a> <span class="about">Looks a value up in a data set by a path of names and conditions - the @ notation as a tag.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:at</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;field@store&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:at</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;path.key=&#039;value&#039;.field@store&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/bool">{pad:bool}</a> <span class="about">Reduces its content or a value to TRUE or FALSE and keeps it under a name, for a later {bool:name} to test.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:bool</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> expression <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:bool</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:bool</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, value<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">bool:name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/bool:name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/content">{pad:content}</a> <span class="about">Keeps a piece of template under a name instead of printing it, for a later {content:name} to render.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:content</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:content</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:content</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:content</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:content</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/count">{pad:count}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content only when a named data set or page array holds at least one element.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:count</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:count</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:count</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:count</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/decrement">{pad:decrement}</a> <span class="about">Takes one off a variable, starting it at -1 when it does not exist yet.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:decrement</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/increment">{pad:increment}</a> <span class="about">Adds one to a variable, starting it at 1 when it does not exist yet.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:increment</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/set">{pad:set}</a> <span class="about">Assigns one or more variables, as globals that outlive the level the tag stands in.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:set</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span> = value<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:set</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span> = value, <span class="hl-var">$other</span> = value<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="database">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Database</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/array">{pad:array}</a> <span class="about">Runs a SQL query on the application database and repeats its content once per row.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:array</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;* from table where ...&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:array</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:array</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;* from orders&quot;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;orders&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:array</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/check">{pad:check}</a> <span class="about">Asks the application database whether a row exists, and renders its content only when one does.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:check</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;table where ...&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:check</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:check</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;users where email=&#039;x@y.z&#039;&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:check</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/collection">{pad:collection}</a> <span class="about">Reads a folder of Markdown files from _content/ and repeats its content once per file, the front matter as fields.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:collection</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:collection</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:collection</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">slug</span>=<span class="hl-var">$slug</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:collection</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:collection</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, html<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:collection</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/field">{pad:field}</a> <span class="about">Runs a SQL query on the application database and prints the single value it answers.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:field</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;column from table where ...&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:field</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;count(*) from orders&quot;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;orderCount&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/record">{pad:record}</a> <span class="about">Runs a SQL query on the application database and renders its content with the first row&#039;s columns as fields.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:record</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;* from table where ...&quot;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:record</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:record</span> <span class="hl-str">&quot;* from users where id=5&quot;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;user&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:record</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="pages">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Pages and execution</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/ajax">{pad:ajax}</a> <span class="about">Writes an empty div and a script that has the browser fetch another page into it after the page has loaded.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:ajax</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:ajax</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">fragment</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;fragmentName&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:ajax</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">app</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;other&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:ajax</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$colour</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;red&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/code">{pad:code}</a> <span class="about">Runs its content as PAD source in a separate engine pass and outputs what that pass produced.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:code</span> sandbox<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:code</span> reset<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:code</span> clean<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:code</span> <span class="hl-opt">function</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:code</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:echo</span> <span class="hl-var">$snippet</span> | code<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/get">{pad:get}</a> <span class="about">Fetches another page of the application over HTTP, as a second request, and inserts its output.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:get</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:get</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$colour</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;red&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/live">{pad:live}</a> <span class="about">A region of the page that re-renders on the server when something in it is clicked, submitted or changed, and swaps itself in.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:live</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-htm">&lt;button</span> <span class="hl-opt">pad-click</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;event&quot;</span> <span class="hl-opt">pad-value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;value&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/button</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:live</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:live</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-htm">&lt;form</span> <span class="hl-opt">pad-submit</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;event&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/form</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:live</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:live</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-htm">&lt;select</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;field&quot;</span> <span class="hl-opt">pad-change</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;event&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/select</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:live</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pad">{pad:pad}</a> <span class="about">A tag with no behaviour of its own: its content renders once, or per row with data=, carrying the generic options.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pad</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pad</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pad</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;store&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pad</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pad</span> <span class="hl-var">$name</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pad</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pad</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pad</span> | upper<span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/page">{pad:page}</a> <span class="about">Renders another page of the application - its PHP and its template - in place of the tag.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;dir/name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;products/42&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">include</span>=false<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, sandbox<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, reset<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, clean<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">function</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:page</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">app</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;other&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ajax</span>=false<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/restart">{pad:restart}</a> <span class="about">Abandons the page being built and renders another page in its place, in the same request.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:restart</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:restart</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$message</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;Saved&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sandbox">{pad:sandbox}</a> <span class="about">Runs its content as PAD source in an isolated pass that cannot see or leave behind anything of the page.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:sandbox</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:sandbox</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:sandbox</span> <span class="hl-opt">function</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:sandbox</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:echo</span> <span class="hl-var">$snippet</span> | sandbox<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="navigation">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Navigation</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pager">{pad:pager}</a> <span class="about">Page links for a tag written with the page option: first and last, a window round the current page, previous and next.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pager</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pager</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">window</span>=<span class="hl-num">2</span>, <span class="hl-opt">query</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;p&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pager</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$kind</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$page</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$label</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$href</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pager</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/redirect">{pad:redirect}</a> <span class="about">Sends the browser to another page or address with a 302 and ends the request.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:redirect</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:redirect</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;orders/list&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$status</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;open&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:redirect</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://example.com/&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sitemap">{pad:sitemap}</a> <span class="about">Lists the pages of the application, read from the file tree, as rows with page, url and lastmod.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:sitemap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$page</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$url</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$lastmod</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:sitemap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:sitemap</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;dir&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:sitemap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="forms">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Forms and security</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/csrf">{pad:csrf}</a> <span class="about">The hidden form field holding the CSRF token of the visitor&#039;s session, or the bare token.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:csrf</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:csrf</span> token<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/flash">{pad:flash}</a> <span class="about">The flash messages of this request - set with padFlash on the page before - one occurrence each.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:flash</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$message</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$type</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:flash</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:flash</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:flash</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:flash</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;error&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$message</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:flash</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/form">{pad:form}</a> <span class="about">A form that posts back to the page, carrying the session&#039;s CSRF token and its own name, its fields refilling when it came back.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:form</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:form</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:form</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">method</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;get&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">class</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;search&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:form</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:form</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">error</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Please correct the errors below.&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:form</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:form</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, client, error<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:form</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/input">{pad:input}</a> <span class="about">A form field with its label that refills from what its form sent and shows the error validation found for it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:input</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:input</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;email&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;email&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;E-mail&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">id</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;mail&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">placeholder</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;you@example.org&#039;</span>, required<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:input</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;terms&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;checkbox&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;I agree&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;yes&#039;</span>, checked<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:input</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;email&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;email&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;E-mail&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rules</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;required|email&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">input:slider</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;volume&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Volume&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">min</span>=<span class="hl-num">0</span>, <span class="hl-opt">max</span>=<span class="hl-num">100</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/nonce">{pad:nonce}</a> <span class="about">This request&#039;s Content-Security-Policy nonce, for a script or style the policy lets run.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:nonce</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/textarea">{pad:textarea}</a> <span class="about">A text area with its label that refills from what its form sent and shows the error validation found for it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:textarea</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:textarea</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Message&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">id</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;msg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">6</span>, required<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:textarea</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Message&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">6</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rules</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;required|max:2000&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/validator">{pad:validator}</a> <span class="about">The browser&#039;s checker of the validation rules as one inline script, for a form a component draws.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:validator</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="access">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Users and access</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/auth">{pad:auth}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when a user is logged in, with the user&#039;s row as its data, and its else half for a guest.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> Hello <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:auth</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/can">{pad:can}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when the user may do something a gate decides, and its else half when not.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:can</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ability&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:can</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:can</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ability&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:can</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">array:rows</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:can</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ability&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$rows</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:can</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/array:rows</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/cannot">{pad:cannot}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when the user may not do something a gate decides - can turned round.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:cannot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ability&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:cannot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:cannot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ability&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:cannot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/feature">{pad:feature}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content while a feature flag is on for this visitor, and its else half while it is off.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:feature</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;flag&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:feature</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:feature</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;flag&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:feature</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/guest">{pad:guest}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when nobody is logged in, and its else half for a user.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:guest</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:guest</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:guest</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:else</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:guest</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/notifications">{pad:notifications}</a> <span class="about">Lists the notifications padNotify kept on the site for the user logged in, unread, each with a link that marks it read.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:notifications</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:notifications</span> <span class="hl-opt">for</span>=<span class="hl-var">$id</span>, all<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$title</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$text</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$created</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$link</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:notifications</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/oauth">{pad:oauth}</a> <span class="about">Writes a &quot;Log in with ...&quot; link styled as a button, for the OAuth login of padOAuthStart and padOAuthUser.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;github&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;oidc&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;?login/company&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Log in with your company account&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="files">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Files</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/dir">{pad:dir}</a> <span class="about">Lists the names in a directory, one occurrence each - the plain version of files.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:dir</span> <span class="hl-var">$path</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$dir</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:dir</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/exists">{pad:exists}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content when a file or directory is there, its else half when not.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:exists</span> <span class="hl-var">$path</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:exists</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:exists</span> <span class="hl-var">$path</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@else@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:exists</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/file">{pad:file}</a> <span class="about">Writes its rendered content to a file under DATA/ instead of to the page.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:file</span> <span class="hl-opt">dir</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;exports&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;orders&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ext</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;csv&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:file</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:file</span> <span class="hl-opt">dir</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;reports&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;daily&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ext</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;html&#039;</span>, date, stamp, <span class="hl-opt">id</span>=<span class="hl-num">8</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:file</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/files">{pad:files}</a> <span class="about">Scans a directory and iterates its entries, with path, file, ext, item and dir for each.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:files</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;dir&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">base</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;app&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$item</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$file</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$ext</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:files</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:files</span> <span class="hl-opt">dir</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;dir&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">base</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;data&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">mask</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;*.txt&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">exclude</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;old*&#039;</span>, recursive, onlyFiles, includeHidden<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:files</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:files</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;dir&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">base</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;app&#039;</span>, onlyDirs<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:files</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="network">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Network and mail</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/curl">{pad:curl}</a> <span class="about">Fetches a URL and answers the body of the response, optionally kept in a cache for a number of seconds.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:curl</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://example.com/data.json&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:curl</span> <span class="hl-opt">url</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;https://example.com/data.json&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ttl</span>=<span class="hl-num">600</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:curl</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://example.com/api&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">post</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;a=1&amp;b=2&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">user</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">password</span>=<span class="hl-var">$secret</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:curl</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;SELF://shop/?orders&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-var">$status</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;open&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:curl</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://example.com/api&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">get</span>=<span class="hl-var">$query</span>, <span class="hl-opt">cookies</span>=<span class="hl-var">$jar</span>, <span class="hl-opt">headers</span>=<span class="hl-var">$headers</span>, <span class="hl-opt">options</span>=<span class="hl-var">$curlOptions</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/mail">{pad:mail}</a> <span class="about">Renders a template, or its own content, to an e-mail with an HTML and a text part, and sends it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:mail</span> <span class="hl-opt">to</span>=<span class="hl-var">$email</span>, <span class="hl-opt">template</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">subject</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Order confirmation&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:mail</span> <span class="hl-opt">to</span>=<span class="hl-var">$email</span>, <span class="hl-opt">subject</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Welcome&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;shop@example.com&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">cc</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;a@example.com&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">bcc</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;b@example.com&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">replyTo</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;help@example.com&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:mail</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="output">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Output control</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/asset">{pad:asset}</a> <span class="about">The address of a file of www/&lt;app&gt;/ with a version of its contents, safe behind a far-future cache header.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:asset</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;file&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:asset</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;file&#039;</span>, tag<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/cache">{pad:cache}</a> <span class="about">Keeps a named section of the page rendered for a number of seconds - the fragment cache.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:cache</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:cache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:cache</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ttl</span>=<span class="hl-num">300</span>, <span class="hl-opt">vary</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:cache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">type:tag</span> <span class="hl-opt">cache</span>=<span class="hl-num">3600</span>, <span class="hl-opt">vary</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/type:tag</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/close">{pad:close}</a> <span class="about">Writes a literal closing brace that the template engine does not read as the end of a tag.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:close</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/echo">{pad:echo}</a> <span class="about">Evaluates an expression and prints the result - the general output tag, and the one to put in front of a pipe.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:echo</span> expression<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:echo</span> expression | pipe | pipe<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/flush">{pad:flush}</a> <span class="about">Sends the page rendered so far to the browser at once, so it can load the stylesheets while a slow part below renders.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:flush</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/foo">{pad:foo}</a> <span class="about">A built-in demo tag that answers a fixed line, there to show how the type prefixes resolve a name.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:foo</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:foo</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/ignore">{pad:ignore}</a> <span class="about">Keeps PAD from reading the braces in its content as tags - for inline JavaScript, CSS, JSON and code samples.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:ignore</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:ignore</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/nocache">{pad:nocache}</a> <span class="about">Marks a part of a cached page or {pad:cache} section that renders again on every request, the hit included.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:nocache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:nocache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:cache</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ttl</span>=<span class="hl-num">300</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:nocache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:nocache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:cache</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/open">{pad:open}</a> <span class="about">Writes a literal opening brace that the template engine does not read as the start of a tag.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:open</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/output">{pad:output}</a> <span class="about">Switches how the running request answers - a web page, the console, a file, a download, JSON or CSV.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:output</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;type&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/reactData">{pad:reactData}</a> <span class="about">Writes the mount point of a React component, its data from a provider in _providers/ as JSON in an attribute.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:reactData</span> <span class="hl-opt">id</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;products&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:reactData</span> <span class="hl-opt">id</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;products&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">provider</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;catalog&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;array&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/spaceless">{pad:spaceless}</a> <span class="about">Takes out the whitespace between HTML tags, and at both ends of the content.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:spaceless</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:spaceless</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/tidy">{pad:tidy}</a> <span class="about">Rewrites the HTML it wraps as tidied, indented markup through PHP&#039;s tidy extension.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tidy</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tidy</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/trans">{pad:trans}</a> <span class="about">Writes the text a key has in the _lang/ catalog of the request&#039;s locale, with plural forms and substitutions.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:trans</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;key&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:trans</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;key&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">count</span>=<span class="hl-var">$n</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:trans</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;key&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span>, <span class="hl-opt">other</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/vite">{pad:vite}</a> <span class="about">Links the scripts and stylesheets of a Vite build by the name of their source - or the dev server while it runs.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:vite</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;src/main.js&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:vite</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;src/main.jsx&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;src/admin.js&#039;</span>, react<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="text">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Text and formatting</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/attrs">{pad:attrs}</a> <span class="about">Writes HTML attributes from values - quoted and escaped, a true boolean attribute bare, a false one left out.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:attrs</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span>, <span class="hl-opt">other</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:attrs</span> required, <span class="hl-opt">aria-expanded</span>=<span class="hl-var">$open</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:attrs</span> <span class="hl-var">$array</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/classes">{pad:classes}</a> <span class="about">Builds a class list from fixed names and names that are in only while their condition holds.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:classes</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;fixed names&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=condition, <span class="hl-opt">other</span>=condition<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:classes</span> <span class="hl-var">$array</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/emoji">{pad:emoji}</a> <span class="about">An emoji by its GitHub or Slack shortcode, as a labelled image a screen reader names.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:emoji</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;rocket&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:emoji</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;:tada:&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Party!&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/excerpt">{pad:excerpt}</a> <span class="about">Cuts a long text to whole words with an ellipsis, centred on the first match of search words, every match marked.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:excerpt</span> <span class="hl-var">$text</span>, <span class="hl-opt">words</span>=<span class="hl-num">40</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:excerpt</span> <span class="hl-var">$text</span>, <span class="hl-opt">words</span>=<span class="hl-num">24</span>, <span class="hl-opt">highlight</span>=<span class="hl-var">$q</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ellipsis</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;...&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:excerpt</span> <span class="hl-var">$html</span>, <span class="hl-opt">words</span>=<span class="hl-num">30</span>, html<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/highlight">{pad:highlight}</a> <span class="about">Colours source code on the server - PAD, PHP, HTML, CSS, JavaScript, JSON, YAML, SQL or shell - with optional line numbers and marked lines.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:highlight</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;lang&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:highlight</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:highlight</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;lang&#039;</span>, lines, <span class="hl-opt">mark</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;3, 5-7&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:highlight</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:highlight</span> <span class="hl-opt">file</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;_data/products.json&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/lorem">{pad:lorem}</a> <span class="about">Placeholder text - Lorem ipsum and Latin words - in words, sentences or paragraphs, the same text for the same seed.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:lorem</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:lorem</span> <span class="hl-opt">words</span>=<span class="hl-num">4</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:lorem</span> <span class="hl-opt">sentences</span>=<span class="hl-num">2</span>, <span class="hl-opt">seed</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:lorem</span> <span class="hl-opt">paragraphs</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span>, <span class="hl-opt">sentences</span>=<span class="hl-num">4</span>, varied<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/markdown">{pad:markdown}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content, or the value of its parameter, as Markdown and writes the HTML.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:markdown</span> <span class="hl-var">$text</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:markdown</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:markdown</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:markdown</span> html<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:markdown</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:markdown</span> ignore<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:markdown</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/source">{pad:source}</a> <span class="about">Shows the files behind a page as tabs, each coloured as highlight colours it - the code shown is the code that runs.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:source</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:source</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;page&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">files</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;_lib/orders.php, www:orders.js&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:source</span> <span class="hl-opt">only</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;_tags/card.pad, www:card.js&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toc">{pad:toc}</a> <span class="about">A table of contents of the page from its own rendered headings, nested and linked - filled in once the page has rendered.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:toc</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:toc</span> <span class="hl-opt">levels</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;2-4&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;On this page&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">list</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;ul&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="graphics">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Charts and codes</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/palette">{pad:palette}</a> <span class="about">The colours of a palette as swatches with their hex values - 32 of them: viridis and its family, the colour-blind safe Okabe-Ito and Tol schemes, ColorBrewer&#039;s scales - the same palettes palette= gives a chart.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:palette</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:palette</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;viridis&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">steps</span>=<span class="hl-num">7</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:palette</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;okabe-ito&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Our chart colours&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:bar</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;sales&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;north, south&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">palette</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;viridis&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="pictures">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Pictures and media</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/country">{pad:country}</a> <span class="about">Writes a country&#039;s flag from its ISO 3166 code or English name, optionally with the name beside it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:country</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;NL&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:country</span> <span class="hl-var">$code</span>, name<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pdf">{pad:pdf}</a> <span class="about">Sends what its content renders as a PDF instead of the page - a writer in plain PHP, the standard fonts, no library.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pdf</span> <span class="hl-opt">file</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A4&#039;</span>, landscape, <span class="hl-opt">font</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;helvetica&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pdf</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pdf</span> preview, <span class="hl-opt">file</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pdf</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pdf</span> download<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/video">{pad:video}</a> <span class="about">Shows a YouTube or Vimeo video as its poster with a play button, loading the player only when pressed - or a video file as a video element.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:video</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=id&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:video</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;https://vimeo.com/id#t=1m30s&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">poster</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/still.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ratio</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;16:9&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:video</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;clips/sunrise.webm&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">poster</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/sunrise.jpg&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="widgets">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Widgets</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/tab">{pad:tab}</a> <span class="about">One item of a tabs, accordion, carousel, wizard, lightbox, splitpane or sortable widget: its label and its content, handed to that owner.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;label&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="layout">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Layouts and components</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/block">{pad:block}</a> <span class="about">A named region of a layout or wrapper - and a page&#039;s override of it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:block</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> default content <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:block</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
