<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:block</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:parent</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> more content <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:block</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/define">{pad:define}</a> <span class="about">Writes a custom tag in the template itself - what a _tags/name.pad file is, without the file.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:define</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">#parm</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-var">@content@</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:define</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:define</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:parms</span> title, <span class="hl-opt">tone</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;info&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;footer&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-var">@content@</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:slot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:define</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">define:name</span> <span class="hl-opt">parm</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">define:name</span> <span class="hl-opt">parm</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> content <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;footer&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>fill<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:slot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/define:name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">define:name</span> ...<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/extends">{pad:extends}</a> <span class="about">Frames the page with a layout instead of its directories&#039; wrappers.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:extends</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;layout&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:extends</span> <span class="hl-var">$layout</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/fragment">{pad:fragment}</a> <span class="about">A named part of the page that a request can ask for alone - for htmx and ajax swaps.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:fragment</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:fragment</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/macro">{pad:macro}</a> <span class="about">A template function: declared parameters, given in order or by name, read as fields inside its body.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:macro</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, required, <span class="hl-opt">optional</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;default&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$required</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$optional</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:macro</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">macro:name</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">macro:name</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-str">&#039;other&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">macro:name</span> <span class="hl-opt">required</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">optional</span>=<span class="hl-var">$x</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">macro:name</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> content for <span class="hl-var">@content@</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/macro:name</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">macro:name</span> ...<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/meta">{pad:meta}</a> <span class="about">The page&#039;s metadata - a title, a layout, a cache time or any value - read while the page is assembled.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:meta</span> <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Title&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=value<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:meta</span> <span class="hl-opt">layout</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;layout&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">cache</span>=<span class="hl-num">600</span>, <span class="hl-opt">access</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;admin&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">sitemap</span>=false<span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/parent">{pad:parent}</a> <span class="about">Inside an overriding block: the content of the block it overrides.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:block</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:parent</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:block</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/parms">{pad:parms}</a> <span class="about">Declares the parameters of a custom tag, with defaults, at the top of its template.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:parms</span> name<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:parms</span> title, <span class="hl-opt">subtitle</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">tone</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;info&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/push">{pad:push}</a> <span class="about">Renders its content and adds it to a named stack, for a stack tag elsewhere in the page to print.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:push</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:push</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:push</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">once</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;key&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:push</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:push</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span>, once<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:push</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/shadow">{pad:shadow}</a> <span class="about">The shadow root of a custom element, rendered on the server as declarative shadow DOM.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:shadow</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:shadow</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:shadow</span> <span class="hl-opt">css</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;file.css, www:other.css&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">mode</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;closed&#039;</span>, focus<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:shadow</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/slot">{pad:slot}</a> <span class="about">A named place in a custom tag&#039;s template - and the fill a caller gives it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> default content <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:slot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-htm">&lt;footer</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-var">@content@</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;/footer</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:slot</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:slot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/stack">{pad:stack}</a> <span class="about">Prints everything pushed to a named stack, from above or below it in the page.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:stack</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/theme">{pad:theme}</a> <span class="about">The colours of the page swapped for a theme - 17 of them, from paper and sepia to a terminal, an ocean and a high-contrast one - or its content alone in a box of the theme.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:theme</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;name&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:theme</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;paper&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:theme</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;terminal&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:theme</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-var">$padTheme</span> = <span class="hl-str">&#039;paper&#039;</span>;</code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/themes">{pad:themes}</a> <span class="about">A switcher of the themes: the visitor&#039;s choice kept in a cookie, the pages built on the frame in that theme from then on, applied the moment it is chosen.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:themes</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:themes</span> <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Colours&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">button</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Use&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="debug">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Debugging and errors</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/assert">{pad:assert}</a> <span class="about">A condition a test run holds the page to; silent everywhere else.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:assert</span> condition<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:assert</span> condition, <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/debug">{pad:debug}</a> <span class="about">Shows values as a collapsible tree in the page, for a local request only, while rendering goes on.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:debug</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:debug</span> <span class="hl-var">$value</span>, <span class="hl-var">$other</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:debug</span> <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-var">$value</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/dump">{pad:dump}</a> <span class="about">Stops the request and shows the debug dump of the engine&#039;s state at that point.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:dump</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/error">{pad:error}</a> <span class="about">Raises a PAD error from the template, handled as the configured error action says.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:error</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/exception">{pad:exception}</a> <span class="about">Throws a real PHP exception from the template, to exercise the error handling.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:exception</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;message&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/exit">{pad:exit}</a> <span class="about">Ends the request at once, with an empty body - the PAD way to stop instead of PHP&#039;s exit.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:exit</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/trace">{pad:trace}</a> <span class="about">Switches the execution trace on for just the part of the page it wraps.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:trace</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:trace</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="sequences">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Sequences</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/flag">{pad:flag}</a> <span class="about">Replaces every value of a sequence with 1 or 0: whether it belongs to a sequence type.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:flag</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;1..10&#039;</span>, even<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:flag</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:flag</span> prime, <span class="hl-opt">from</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">12</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:flag</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">flag:prime</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/flag:prime</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:flag</span> store, prime<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:flag</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/pull">{pad:pull}</a> <span class="about">Iterates a stored sequence, optionally through plays and actions.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pull</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;store&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pull</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pull</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pull</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pull:store</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pull:store</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pull</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;store&#039;</span>, reverse, <span class="hl-opt">first</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pull</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:pull</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;store&#039;</span>, keep, prime<span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:pull</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/resume">{pad:resume}</a> <span class="about">Transforms the sequence pushed last, in place, through an action or a play.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:resume</span> reverse<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:resume</span> <span class="hl-opt">add</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:resume</span> keep, prime<span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:resume</span> <span class="hl-opt">first</span>=<span class="hl-num">3</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diff/json">{diff:json}</a> <span class="about">Structural JSON changes by escaped JSON Pointer path, preserving types.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diff:json</span> <span class="hl-var">$old</span>, <span class="hl-var">$new</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diff/lines">{diff:lines}</a> <span class="about">A line comparison with old and new line numbers and changed words.</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diff/rows">{diff:rows}</a> <span class="about">Records matched by a unique key, with added, removed and changed fields.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diff:rows</span> <span class="hl-var">$old</span>, <span class="hl-var">$new</span>, <span class="hl-opt">key</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;id&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diff/side">{diff:side}</a> <span class="about">Old and new lines side by side with changed words highlighted.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diff:side</span> <span class="hl-var">$old</span>, <span class="hl-var">$new</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diff/words">{diff:words}</a> <span class="about">Changed words marked as escaped deletions and insertions.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diff:words</span> <span class="hl-var">$old</span>, <span class="hl-var">$new</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">table:grouped</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">group</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;region&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">totals</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;amount&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>

      <a class="name" href="?tag/table/matrix">{table:matrix}</a> <span class="about">A cross-tabulation with shaded cells for comparing values or counts.</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">table:pivot</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">row</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;region&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">column</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;quarter&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;amount&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/table/standard">{table:standard}</a> <span class="about">Rows as a formatted table with totals, column filters, sorting and paging.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">table:standard</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/image/figure">{image:figure}</a> <span class="about">A semantic figure with escaped caption text and the shared image/filter pipeline.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:figure</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/avatar-demo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Harbour&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/image/picture">{image:picture}</a> <span class="about">Responsive picture with generated width variants and matching media sources.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:picture</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/avatar-demo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Harbour&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/image/thumbnail">{image:thumbnail}</a> <span class="about">A picture of www/&lt;application&gt;/ as a thumbnail of the size the page shows it at.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photos/harbour.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">400</span>, <span class="hl-opt">height</span>=<span class="hl-num">300</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;The harbour at dawn&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>

<section class="section" id="kind-avatar">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Avatars</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/avatar/geometric">{avatar:geometric}</a> <span class="about">Geometric portraits from a stable hash, in rings, mosaic or beam styles.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">avatar:geometric</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Ada Lovelace&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/avatar/gravatar">{avatar:gravatar}</a> <span class="about">The Gravatar picture of an e-mail address as an &lt;img&gt;, by subs/avatars/_lib/gravatar.php.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">avatar:gravatar</span> <span class="hl-var">$email</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/avatar/identicon">{avatar:identicon}</a> <span class="about">A symmetric 5 by 5 pattern in a colour of its own, drawn from a SHA-256 hash of the value by subs/avatars/_lib/identicon.php.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">avatar:identicon</span> <span class="hl-var">$email</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/avatar/initials">{avatar:initials}</a> <span class="about">An initials avatar as inline SVG, drawn by subs/avatars/_lib/avatar.php.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">avatar:initials</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Herbert Jebbink&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/avatar/mosaic">{avatar:mosaic}</a> <span class="about">A grid of coloured triangles derived deterministically from an identity.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">avatar:mosaic</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Ada Lovelace&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/avatar/photo">{avatar:photo}</a> <span class="about">A local or HTTP(S) photograph with a generated fallback, without server-side fetching.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">avatar:photo</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Ada Lovelace&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/avatar/pixelbot">{avatar:pixelbot}</a> <span class="about">A small robot avatar with deterministic colours and facial details.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">avatar:pixelbot</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Ada Lovelace&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/avatar/rings">{avatar:rings}</a> <span class="about">Concentric coloured arcs derived deterministically from an identity.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">avatar:rings</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Ada Lovelace&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

  <div class="cheat">

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/format/auto">{format:auto}</a> <span class="about">Selects the kind from its parameters or configured default.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">format:auto</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/format:auto</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>

<section class="section" id="kind-chart">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Chart types</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/arc">{chart:arc}</a> <span class="about">Nodes on a line in the order they first appear, a dot as large as its number of links, its name under it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:arc</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/area">{chart:area}</a> <span class="about">Filled areas over the label axis, a line on each and a legend for several value fields.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:area</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/bar">{chart:bar}</a> <span class="about">Columns from a zero baseline.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:bar</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/boxplot">{chart:boxplot}</a> <span class="about">A box per group of label.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:boxplot</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/bubble">{chart:bubble}</a> <span class="about">A scatter whose dots have the area of size.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:bubble</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/bullet">{chart:bullet}</a> <span class="about">A track per row with a thin bar of value and target.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:bullet</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/bump">{chart:bump}</a> <span class="about">Ranks across several periods, with one line per item and missing periods left as gaps.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:bump</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/calendar">{chart:calendar}</a> <span class="about">A square per day, a column per week.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:calendar</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/candlestick">{chart:candlestick}</a> <span class="about">A candle per row (label the day).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">chart:candlestick</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">value</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;field&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>

      <a class="name" href="?tag/chart/stream">{chart:stream}</a> <span class="about">A streamgraph: the value fields stacked in smooth curves around a wandering middle.</span>

<section class="section" id="kind-game">

  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/game/hanoi">{game:hanoi}</a> <span class="about">The towers of Hanoi: a stack of discs, the largest at the bottom, moves from the first peg to the last.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">game:hanoi</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">game:morris</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/game/nim">{game:nim}</a> <span class="about">A few heaps of matches; each turn takes as many as you like, at least one, from one heap, and who takes the last one wins.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">game:nim</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/game/nonogram">{game:nonogram}</a> <span class="about">A picture to uncover: the numbers beside each row and over each column are the runs of filled squares in it, in order.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">game:nonogram</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

    <div class="cheat-item">

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">game:pegsolitaire</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/game/quiz">{game:quiz}</a> <span class="about">Questions with answers to choose from, one at a time, a point for each right one and the right answer shown after a wrong one.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">game:quiz</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/game/reversi">{game:reversi}</a> <span class="about">On a board of eight by eight a disc is placed so that it closes a line of the other colour&#039;s discs between itself and one of its own, and every disc it closes in turns over.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">game:reversi</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/game/snake">{game:snake}</a> <span class="about">Steer a snake round a field with the arrow keys, by swiping or with the buttons, to eat the apples.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">game:snake</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">

    <div class="cheat-item">

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/game/tictactoe">{game:tictactoe}</a> <span class="about">Three in a row on a board of three by three, against the computer, which looks every game through to its end and so never loses.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">game:tictactoe</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

    <div class="cheat-item">

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/mandelbrot">{toy:mandelbrot}</a> <span class="about">The Mandelbrot set, the points c of the plane where z = z&sup2; + c stays near home, its edge coloured by how fast the rest escape.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:mandelbrot</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/optics">{toy:optics}</a> <span class="about">Trace rays through a movable light source, lenses and a plane mirror on an interactive optics bench.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:optics</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/orbits">{toy:orbits}</a> <span class="about">Planets and stars pulling at each other by gravity, every body on every other, drawing their paths as they go.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:orbits</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:pathfinding</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/pendulum">{toy:pendulum}</a> <span class="about">A double pendulum: a second pendulum hung from the end of the first, the classic chaotic system.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:pendulum</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/percolation">{toy:percolation}</a> <span class="about">Water finding its way through a porous grid, site percolation.</span>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:reactiondiffusion</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/ripples">{toy:ripples}</a> <span class="about">A ripple tank: waves spread over water, bounce off walls and run through each other, crest on crest and trough on trough.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:ripples</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/sand">{toy:sand}</a> <span class="about">A falling sand box: paint with sand that piles up, water that runs and finds its level, walls, wood that burns.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:sand</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/sorting">{toy:sorting}</a> <span class="about">Sorting algorithms at work on a row of bars.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:sorting</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/spirograph">{toy:spirograph}</a> <span class="about">A toothed wheel rolls round inside a ring, a pen in one of its holes, and draws the curve.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:spirograph</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/toy/starfield">{toy:starfield}</a> <span class="about">Flying through space: stars come out of the dark ahead and rush past, faster the nearer they get.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">toy:starfield</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="kind-diagram">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Diagram types</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/auto">{diagram:auto}</a> <span class="about">Select a diagram from its Mermaid header, or draw a flowchart without one.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:auto</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:auto</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/block">{diagram:block}</a> <span class="about">Labelled nodes placed on an explicit grid, with row and column spans and routed connections.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:block</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:block</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/c4">{diagram:c4}</a> <span class="about">Software architecture with people, systems, containers and components inside optional nested boundaries.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:c4</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:c4</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/class">{diagram:class}</a> <span class="about">UML classes, a box each of the name, the fields and the methods, and the relations between them, the Mermaid way.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:class</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:class</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/er">{diagram:er}</a> <span class="about">Entities as tables of their attributes and the relations between them, the feet of a crow at each end for how many, the Mermaid way.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:er</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:er</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/fishbone">{diagram:fishbone}</a> <span class="about">The possible causes of an effect, as Ishikawa drew them.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:fishbone</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:fishbone</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/flowchart">{diagram:flowchart}</a> <span class="about">Nodes and the edges between them, a line a chain.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:flowchart</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:flowchart</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/gitgraph">{diagram:gitgraph}</a> <span class="about">The commits of a repository on their branches, the branches leaving and the merges joining, the Mermaid way.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:gitgraph</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:gitgraph</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/journey">{diagram:journey}</a> <span class="about">The steps a person takes, how each felt and who took it, the Mermaid way.</span>

  ...
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:journey</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/diagram/mindmap">{diagram:mindmap}</a> <span class="about">A subject in the middle and its branches round it, the hierarchy read from the indentation, the Mermaid way.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">diagram:mindmap</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/diagram:packet</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>

<section class="section" id="kind-widget">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Widgets</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/accordion">{widget:accordion}</a> <span class="about">Every {pad:tab} a &lt;details&gt; with its label as the &lt;summary&gt;.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:accordion</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Question&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:accordion</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/breadcrumb">{widget:breadcrumb}</a> <span class="about">Where the page stands: a &lt;nav&gt; with an ordered list of links from the application&#039;s home to the page, the last one the page itself.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:breadcrumb</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/carousel">{widget:carousel}</a> <span class="about">Every {pad:tab} a slide of a row that scrolls and snaps sideways, with previous and next links on each slide and a dot per slide below.</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/splitpane">{widget:splitpane}</a> <span class="about">Two panes side by side, or stacked, with a bar between them that resizes them.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:splitpane</span> <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-num">30</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Folders&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Message&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:splitpane</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/tabs">{widget:tabs}</a> <span class="about">Tabs without JavaScript: a radio button and a label per tab, the CSS shows the panel of the checked one.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:tabs</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Overview&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Specs&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:tabs</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/toast">{widget:toast}</a> <span class="about">A notice the visitor dismisses.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:toast</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Saved&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>Your changes are kept.<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:toast</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:toast</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Saved&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/tooltip">{widget:tooltip}</a> <span class="about">A short tip shown while the pointer is on its trigger or the trigger has the focus.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:tooltip</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Billed every month&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>Starter plan<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:tooltip</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:tooltip</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;What this means&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/treeview">{widget:treeview}</a> <span class="about">A hierarchical navigation list with expandable branches and keyboard navigation.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:treeview</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;nodes&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/widget/wizard">{widget:wizard}</a> <span class="about">A form or a story in steps, one shown at a time.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">widget:wizard</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Address&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:tab</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Payment&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/pad:tab</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/widget:wizard</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>

<section class="section" id="kind-barcode">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Barcode types</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/auto">{barcode:auto}</a> <span class="about">Select EAN-13 for twelve or thirteen digits, otherwise Code 128.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:auto</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/aztec">{barcode:aztec}</a> <span class="about">Aztec Code (ISO/IEC 24778), the square code of train tickets and boarding passes, a bullseye in its middle.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:aztec</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/bookland">{barcode:bookland}</a> <span class="about">The barcode of a book, its ISBN as the EAN-13 called Bookland EAN.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:bookland</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/codabar">{barcode:codabar}</a> <span class="about">Codabar, the barcode of libraries, blood banks and parcel services.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:codabar</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/code11">{barcode:code11}</a> <span class="about">Code 11: digits/hyphens, checks=0/1/2.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:code11</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/code128">{barcode:code128}</a> <span class="about">Code 128, the code of parcels and shipping labels.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:code128</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/code39">{barcode:code39}</a> <span class="about">Code 39, the barcode of industry and of warehouse labels.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:code39</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/code93">{barcode:code93}</a> <span class="about">Code 93, the denser successor of Code 39.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:code93</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/databar">{barcode:databar}</a> <span class="about">The GS1 DataBar Omnidirectional of ISO/IEC 24724, the small barcode of fresh food, loose produce and coupons.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:databar</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/datamatrix">{barcode:datamatrix}</a> <span class="about">Data Matrix ECC 200 (ISO/IEC 16022), the square code of parts, letters and medicine packs.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:datamatrix</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/ean13">{barcode:ean13}</a> <span class="about">EAN-13, the number of a product in a shop.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:ean13</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/ean8">{barcode:ean8}</a> <span class="about">EAN-8, the short EAN of a small package.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:ean8</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/gs1128">{barcode:gs1128}</a> <span class="about">GS1-128, the barcode of the logistics label.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:gs1128</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/itf">{barcode:itf}</a> <span class="about">Interleaved 2 of 5, the barcode of digits on cartons, tickets and film.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:itf</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/itf14">{barcode:itf14}</a> <span class="about">ITF-14, the GS1 number of a carton or a pallet of products.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:itf14</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/microqr">{barcode:microqr}</a> <span class="about">Micro QR versions M1 through M4, with explicit encoding mode, error correction and mask options.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:microqr</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/msi">{barcode:msi}</a> <span class="about">MSI, the Plessey code of shelf labels.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:msi</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/pdf417">{barcode:pdf417}</a> <span class="about">PDF417 (ISO/IEC 15438), the stacked code of boarding passes, driving licences and shipping documents.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:pdf417</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/qr">{barcode:qr}</a> <span class="about">A QR code (ISO/IEC 18004), the code a phone camera reads.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:qr</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/upca">{barcode:upca}</a> <span class="about">UPC-A, the product number of North America.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:upca</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/barcode/upce">{barcode:upce}</a> <span class="about">UPC-E, the UPC-A of a small package with its zeros left out.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">barcode:upce</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>

  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">icon:zoom</span>-out <span class="hl-opt">size</span>=<span class="hl-num">24</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="kind-sequence">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Sequence types</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/abundant">{sequence:abundant}</a> <span class="about">Numbers whose proper divisors add up to more than themselves (12, 18, 20, 24, 30, ...).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:abundant</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$abundant</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:abundant</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/add">{sequence:add}</a> <span class="about">Addition (with N = 3: 4, 5, 6, 7, ...).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:add</span> <span class="hl-num">3</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$add</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:add</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/amicable">{sequence:amicable}</a> <span class="about">Both numbers of every amicable pair, each the sum of the other&#039;s proper divisors (220, 284, 1184, 1210, ...).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:amicable</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$amicable</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:amicable</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/and">{sequence:and}</a> <span class="about">Bitwise AND (with N = 3: 1, 2, 3, 0, ...).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:and</span> <span class="hl-num">3</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$and</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:and</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/antiprime">{sequence:antiprime}</a> <span class="about">Antiprime (highly composite) numbers (1, 2, 4, 6, 12, 24, 36, ...) - walked from one to the next, so all 167 an integer holds are within reach; with `try=`, `skip=` or `increment=` every value is a candidate, as for the other types.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:antiprime</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$antiprime</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:antiprime</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/automorphic">{sequence:automorphic}</a> <span class="about">Numbers whose square ends in the number itself (0, 1, 5, 6, 25, 76, 376, 625, ...).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:automorphic</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$automorphic</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:automorphic</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/bell">{sequence:bell}</a> <span class="about">Bell numbers (1, 2, 5, 15, 52, 203, ...).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:bell</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$bell</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:bell</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/biquadratic">{sequence:biquadratic}</a> <span class="about">Biquadratic numbers (1, 16, 81, 256, ...).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:biquadratic</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$biquadratic</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:biquadratic</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/carmichael">{sequence:carmichael}</a> <span class="about">Carmichael numbers, the composites Fermat&#039;s test cannot tell from primes (561, 1105, 1729, ...) - seven below the default 10,000 candidates, `try=` reaches more.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:carmichael</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$carmichael</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:carmichael</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/catalan">{sequence:catalan}</a> <span class="about">Catalan numbers (1, 2, 5, 14, 42, 132, ...).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:catalan</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$catalan</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:catalan</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sequence/caterer">{sequence:caterer}</a> <span class="about">Lazy caterer&#039;s sequence (2, 4, 7, 11, 16, 22, ...).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sequence:caterer</span> <span class="hl-opt">rows</span>=<span class="hl-num">10</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-var">$caterer</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sequence:caterer</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>

<section class="section" id="kind-sound">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Sounds</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/applause">{sound:applause}</a> <span class="about">A short round of applause from a small crowd that is made up.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:applause</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/beep">{sound:beep}</a> <span class="about">The plain beep of a device.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:beep</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/bell">{sound:bell}</a> <span class="about">A struck bell that rings for two seconds.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:bell</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/chime">{sound:chime}</a> <span class="about">Two soft bells a fifth apart, the second a moment after the first.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:chime</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/clap">{sound:clap}</a> <span class="about">A hand clap as a drum machine makes it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:clap</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/click">{sound:click}</a> <span class="about">The short dry click of a switch or a key, a burst of noise and a tap of tone.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:click</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/coin">{sound:coin}</a> <span class="about">The coin of an old video game.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:coin</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/countdown">{sound:countdown}</a> <span class="about">Ready, set, go: three short beeps half a second apart, then a long one an octave higher.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:countdown</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/error">{sound:error}</a> <span class="about">Two low notes falling a tritone, buzzing and dull.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:error</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/fanfare">{sound:fanfare}</a> <span class="about">You won: three quick notes on G, then a held C major chord, like brass.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:fanfare</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/gameover">{sound:gameover}</a> <span class="about">Better luck next time: four notes sinking a semitone each, the last one long and wobbling, like a muted horn.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:gameover</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/hihat">{sound:hihat}</a> <span class="about">A closed hi-hat: a very short hiss of noise, all its low end filtered away.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:hihat</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/jump">{sound:jump}</a> <span class="about">The hop of a platform game.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:jump</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/kick">{sound:kick}</a> <span class="about">A bass drum: a sine that drops from 150 Hz to 45 in a tenth of a second, with a little click of noise on the beat.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:kick</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/laser">{sound:laser}</a> <span class="about">The zap of a space game: a square wave diving from high to low.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:laser</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/notify">{sound:notify}</a> <span class="about">A gentle ding-dong, G then C above it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:notify</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/pop">{sound:pop}</a> <span class="about">A bubble that bursts: a sine swept quickly upward, gone as soon as it is there.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:pop</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/powerup">{sound:powerup}</a> <span class="about">Collected something that makes you stronger.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:powerup</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/shaker">{sound:shaker}</a> <span class="about">A shaker moved forth and back.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:shaker</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/snare">{sound:snare}</a> <span class="about">A snare drum: a burst of noise, highpassed for the rattle of the snares, over a short falling tone for the drum&#039;s body.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:snare</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:softsuccess</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/sparkle">{sound:sparkle}</a> <span class="about">A little magic: high bell tones of E major tumbling over each other, each ringing on under the next, with a breath of bright noise.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:sparkle</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/success">{sound:success}</a> <span class="about">A quick rising major arpeggio, C E G C.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:success</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/tick">{sound:tick}</a> <span class="about">The tick of a clock: a high wooden tap, shorter and brighter than a click.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:tick</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:toggleoff</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:toggleon</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:warning</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:waterdrop</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/sound/whoosh">{sound:whoosh}</a> <span class="about">Air rushing past: noise through a bandpass filter that sweeps up from low to high, swelling and fading.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sound:whoosh</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

<section class="section" id="kind-notation">

  <div class="cheat">

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notation:braille</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notation:karnaugh</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/notation/math">{notation:math}</a> <span class="about">A formula written in AsciiMath, the notation of asciimath.org, as MathML.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notation:math</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>

    <div class="cheat-item">

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notation:morse</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/notation/semaphore">{notation:semaphore}</a> <span class="about">A text in flag semaphore.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notation:semaphore</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
