      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notation:signals</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/notation/siteswap">{notation:siteswap}</a> <span class="about">A juggling pattern in siteswap notation, each throw the number of beats until the ball is thrown again.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notation:siteswap</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/notation/tablature">{notation:tablature}</a> <span class="about">A tab for a guitar, a bass, a ukulele or any fretted instrument, written as players write it in plain text and drawn as it is printed.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notation:tablature</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/notation/xiangqi">{notation:xiangqi}</a> <span class="about">A Chinese chess position on its nine-file, ten-rank board, with river and palaces.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">notation:xiangqi</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;text&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:testimonials</span> <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;heading&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">section:item</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Ada Lovelace&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">role</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Analyst&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>The quote<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:item</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span> ... <span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/section:testimonials</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="kind-effect">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Effects</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/confetti">{effect:confetti}</a> <span class="about">A burst of confetti from where it stands.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:confetti</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:confetti</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;button</span> <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;button&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>Celebrate<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/button</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:confetti</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">

    <div class="cheat-item">

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:decipher</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>ACCESS GRANTED<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:decipher</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/encircle">{effect:encircle}</a> <span class="about">A ring drawn round a word or a short phrase as a pen circles it on paper.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:encircle</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>the one word<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:encircle</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/flipnumber">{effect:flipnumber}</a> <span class="about">A number whose digits animate when its value changes, with readable text and reduced-motion support.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:flipnumber</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:flipnumber</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/glitch">{effect:glitch}</a> <span class="about">A digital glitch: for a moment the text tears into slices that jump aside in two colours, then it stands still again.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:glitch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>SIGNAL LOST<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:glitch</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/gradient">{effect:gradient}</a> <span class="about">Text painted with a gradient.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:gradient</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>Build pages, not plumbing<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:gradient</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">

      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:highlighter</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>the one thing to remember<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:highlighter</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:highlighter</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;this&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/marquee">{effect:marquee}</a> <span class="about">A band whose content scrolls by without end, a logo strip, a ticker.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:marquee</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;span</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>PHP<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/span</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-htm">&lt;span</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>Go<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/span</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span> <span class="hl-htm">&lt;span</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>Rust<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/span</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:marquee</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/nudge">{effect:nudge}</a> <span class="about">A short movement that draws the eye to something, then lets it be.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:nudge</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;a</span> <span class="hl-opt">class</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;button&quot;</span> <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;?signup&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>Try it free<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/a</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:nudge</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/reveal">{effect:reveal}</a> <span class="about">A block that comes into view as it is scrolled to.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:reveal</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;div</span> <span class="hl-opt">class</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;card&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/div</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:reveal</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/ripple">{effect:ripple}</a> <span class="about">An activation ripple around an existing button or link, preserving its normal action.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:ripple</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>...<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:ripple</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/rotator">{effect:rotator}</a> <span class="about">One place in a line where its phrases take turns.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:rotator</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>teams, makers, you<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:rotator</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/scrollprogress">{effect:scrollprogress}</a> <span class="about">A thin bar along the top of the window that fills as the reader scrolls the page, from its top to its end.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:scrollprogress</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/shimmer">{effect:shimmer}</a> <span class="about">A band of light that sweeps across the letters and rests, as on something freshly polished.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:shimmer</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>New in this release<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:shimmer</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/skeleton">{effect:skeleton}</a> <span class="about">A placeholder in the shape of what is still on its way, a sheen sweeping over it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:skeleton</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:skeleton</span> <span class="hl-opt">lines</span>=<span class="hl-num">4</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/spinner">{effect:spinner}</a> <span class="about">A loading indicator, the one effect for every loader.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:spinner</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:spinner</span> <span class="hl-opt">style</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;dots&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">label</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Saving&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:spinner</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>Loading the orders<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:spinner</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/spotlight">{effect:spotlight}</a> <span class="about">A block lit by a soft light behind its content.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:spotlight</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;h2</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>Introducing PAD<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/h2</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;p</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/p</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:spotlight</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/strike">{effect:strike}</a> <span class="about">A line drawn through the words from left to right, once.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:strike</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>€<span class="hl-num">49</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:strike</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/tilt">{effect:tilt}</a> <span class="about">A card that leans in 3D when the pointer is on it or the focus is in it, and settles again when it leaves.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:tilt</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span><span class="hl-htm">&lt;div</span> <span class="hl-opt">class</span>=<span class="hl-str">&quot;card&quot;</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span>...<span class="hl-htm">&lt;/div</span><span class="hl-htm">&gt;</span><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:tilt</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/typewriter">{effect:typewriter}</a> <span class="about">A line of text typed out letter by letter behind a caret, once, then the caret blinks a few times and stays.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:typewriter</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>Hello, world<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:typewriter</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:typewriter</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Hello, world&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/underline">{effect:underline}</a> <span class="about">A line drawn under the words from left to right, once.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:underline</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>the terms<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:underline</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:underline</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;this&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/effect/wave">{effect:wave}</a> <span class="about">The letters rise and fall one after the other, a wave running through the word.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:wave</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>Hello there<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/effect:wave</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">effect:wave</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;Hello there&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>

<section class="section" id="kind-jsonld">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>JSON-LD kinds</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/article">{jsonld:article}</a> <span class="about">An Article: a post of a blog, a news item, a page of a magazine, its headline, who wrote it and when.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:article</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;post&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:article</span> <span class="hl-opt">headline</span>=<span class="hl-var">$title</span>, <span class="hl-opt">author</span>=<span class="hl-var">$author</span>, <span class="hl-opt">published</span>=<span class="hl-var">$date</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/book">{jsonld:book}</a> <span class="about">Book metadata with named authors, a validated ISBN and edition details.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:book</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/breadcrumb">{jsonld:breadcrumb}</a> <span class="about">A BreadcrumbList: where the page stands, from the home page down, each step a ListItem numbered by its position.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:breadcrumb</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:breadcrumb</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;trail&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/business">{jsonld:business}</a> <span class="about">A LocalBusiness: a shop, a practice, a garage.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:business</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;shop&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:business</span> <span class="hl-opt">type</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Bakery&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;The Loaf&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">street</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Main Street 1&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">city</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Utrecht&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">country</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;NL&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">hours</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Mo-Fr 08:00-18:00; Sa 08:00-16:00&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/course">{jsonld:course}</a> <span class="about">A Course: a series of lessons, what it teaches and who gives it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:course</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;course&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:course</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;PAD in a day&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">description</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;The template language from the first tag to a shop.&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">provider</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;PAD Academy&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/dataset">{jsonld:dataset}</a> <span class="about">Dataset metadata with a named creator, license and explicitly validated downloads.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:dataset</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/event">{jsonld:event}</a> <span class="about">An Event: a concert, a course, a meeting, when it starts and where, and the tickets for it.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:event</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;concert&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:event</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;PAD day&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">starts</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;2026-11-20 19:30&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">place</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;The Hall&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">address</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Main Street 1, Utrecht&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/faq">{jsonld:faq}</a> <span class="about">An FAQPage: the questions of a page and their answers, each a Question with its acceptedAnswer.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:faq</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;questions&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/itemlist">{jsonld:itemlist}</a> <span class="about">An ItemList: the page that lists pages of one kind.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:itemlist</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;recipes&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/job">{jsonld:job}</a> <span class="about">A JobPosting: a vacancy, what the work is, who offers it, where and for what pay.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:job</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;vacancy&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:job</span> <span class="hl-opt">title</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Baker&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">description</span>=<span class="hl-var">$text</span>, <span class="hl-opt">posted</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;2026-11-01&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">company</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;The Loaf&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">city</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Utrecht&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">country</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;NL&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">employment</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;full time&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">salary</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;3200-3800&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">currency</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;EUR&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">per</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;month&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/organization">{jsonld:organization}</a> <span class="about">An Organization: the company, the club or the project behind the site, its logo and its profiles elsewhere.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:organization</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;PAD&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">url</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;https://pad.example&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">logo</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;logo.png&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/person">{jsonld:person}</a> <span class="about">A Person: the author of a site, a speaker, a member of a team.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:person</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Ann Smith&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">job</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Engineer&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">profiles</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;https://github.com/ann&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/product">{jsonld:product}</a> <span class="about">A Product: a thing for sale, what it is and what it costs.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:product</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;product&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:product</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Kettle&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">price</span>=<span class="hl-var">$price</span>, <span class="hl-opt">currency</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;EUR&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">availability</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;InStock&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/profile">{jsonld:profile}</a> <span class="about">A ProfilePage: the page of one person, or one organization, on a site of many.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:profile</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;member&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:profile</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Ann Smith&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">handle</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;ann&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">image</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;avatars/ann.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">followers</span>=<span class="hl-var">$followers</span>, <span class="hl-opt">created</span>=<span class="hl-var">$joined</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/recipe">{jsonld:recipe}</a> <span class="about">A Recipe: a dish, what goes in it, how it is made and how long that takes.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:recipe</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;recipe&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:recipe</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Pea soup&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">image</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;soup.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">prep</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;20 min&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">cook</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;1 h 30 min&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">yield</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;4 servings&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/restaurant">{jsonld:restaurant}</a> <span class="about">A Restaurant: a local business that serves food and drink, and what it serves.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:restaurant</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;restaurant&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:restaurant</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;De Ketel&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">cuisine</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Dutch&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">menu</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;?menu&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">street</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Oudegracht 1&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">city</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Utrecht&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">country</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;NL&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">hours</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Tu-Su 17:00-23:00&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">prices</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;$$&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/service">{jsonld:service}</a> <span class="about">Service metadata with a provider, served places and optional priced offer.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:service</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;rows&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/software">{jsonld:software}</a> <span class="about">A SoftwareApplication: a program, an app, a web application.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:software</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;app&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:software</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Pocket Ledger&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">os</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Android 12, iOS 17&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">category</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;finance&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">price</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;0&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">rating</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;4.6&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">ratings</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;1523&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/video">{jsonld:video}</a> <span class="about">A VideoObject: a video of the page, what it shows, when it was put there, how long it runs and where it plays.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:video</span> <span class="hl-opt">data</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;video&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span>
<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:video</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;Making pea soup&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">thumbnail</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;soup.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">uploaded</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;2026-11-01T10:00:00+01:00&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">duration</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;4 min 12 s&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">player</span>=<span class="hl-var">$embed</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?tag/jsonld/website">{jsonld:website}</a> <span class="about">A WebSite: the site itself, its name and its address, and how to search it, which a search engine may offer as a search box of its own.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">jsonld:website</span> <span class="hl-opt">name</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;PAD&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">search</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;?search&amp;q=&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="subsystem-rule">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Validation rules</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/accepted">rule / accepted</a> <span class="about">A box that must be ticked, terms and conditions: 1, on, yes or true, in any case.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;accepted&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/after">rule / after</a> <span class="about">After:other - a moment strictly after another, read as before: reads it: after:start, the other field of that name, or after:2026-01-01, a date written in the rule.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;after:other&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/after_or_equal">rule / after_or_equal</a> <span class="about">After_or_equal:other - a moment after another or the same one, as after: reads them: the end of a trip of one day, after_or_equal:start, or after_or_equal:2026-01-01, a date written in the rule.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;after_or_equal:other&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/alpha">rule / alpha</a> <span class="about">Letters only, of any script, with the marks that go with them: Jos&eacute; and Zo&euml; pass, a space, a digit or a dash does not.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;alpha&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/alphanum">rule / alphanum</a> <span class="about">Letters and digits only, of any script: a user name, a code without signs.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;alphanum&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/before">rule / before</a> <span class="about">Before:other - a moment strictly before another: before:end, the other field of that name, or before:2027-01-01, a date written in the rule - one that reads as a date (four digits, a dash, a digit) is a date, whatever field the post has of that name.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;before:other&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/before_or_equal">rule / before_or_equal</a> <span class="about">Before_or_equal:other - a moment before another or the same one, as before: reads them: a start no later than the end, before_or_equal:end, or before_or_equal:2026-12-31, a date written in the rule.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;before_or_equal:other&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/between">rule / between</a> <span class="about">Between:a,b - min:a and max:b in one rule: from a to b characters long, or on a number field a value from a to b.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;between:1,10&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/color">rule / color</a> <span class="about">A colour written in hexadecimal, #rgb or #rrggbb, in either case: what &lt;input type=&quot;color&quot;&gt; posts, and what a style sheet can be handed back as it is.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;color&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/date">rule / date</a> <span class="about">A day of a month of a year, in any form date_parse reads without an error, that checkdate takes: 2026-02-30 is none.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;date&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/different">rule / different</a> <span class="about">Different:field - not the same text as another field of the post, the mirror of same: a new password that is not the one in use, different:current_password.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;different:other&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/digits">rule / digits</a> <span class="about">Digits, digits:n - the digits 0 to 9 and nothing else, and with n exactly n of them: a postal code of four, a PIN of six.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;digits:4&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/distinct">rule / distinct</a> <span class="about">No value is in a list twice (a field named guests[]): the addresses of the people invited, each once.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;distinct&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/email">rule / email</a> <span class="about">An e-mail address as PHP&#039;s FILTER_VALIDATE_EMAIL takes one; the browser&#039;s half carries the same pattern.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;email&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/ends_with">rule / ends_with</a> <span class="about">Ends_with:a,b - the value ends with one of the texts of the list, as written: ends_with:.pdf,.txt for a file name, ends_with:@example.com for an address of the house.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ends_with:.jpg,.png&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/gt">rule / gt</a> <span class="about">Gt:other - more than another field, or than a number written in the rule: a highest price above the lowest, gt:min_price, an amount above nothing, gt:0 - where min:0 takes 0.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;gt:0&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/gte">rule / gte</a> <span class="about">Gte:other - at least another field, or a number written in the rule: a highest price no lower than the lowest, gte:min_price.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;gte:0&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/iban">rule / iban</a> <span class="about">An international bank account number whose check digits hold: the country&#039;s two letters, two check digits and 11 to 30 letters and digits, the spaces it is often written with left out, in either case; moved the first four to the end and read with A as 10 to Z as 35, the number leaves 1 when divided by 97 (ISO 13616).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;iban&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
