    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/timezone">rule / timezone</a> <span class="about">The browser receives this exact runtime list through the client argument hook.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;timezone&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/ulid">rule / ulid</a> <span class="about">Validation only: canonical uppercase Crockford ULID, overflow excluded.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ulid&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/uppercase">rule / uppercase</a> <span class="about">Nothing that has an upper case is written in lower case: a country code, a licence plate.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;uppercase&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/url">rule / url</a> <span class="about">A web address: what FILTER_VALIDATE_URL takes, and http or https - a javascript: or file: address is a URL too, and no address a visitor&#039;s link should hold.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;url&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/rule/uuid">rule / uuid</a> <span class="about">A UUID in its usual form, 8-4-4-4-12 hexadecimal digits, of any version - what padStrUuid makes - in either case.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-var">$errors</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padValidate</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;value&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;uuid&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-var">$values</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="subsystem-oauth">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>OAuth providers</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/apple">oauth / apple</a> <span class="about">Sign in with Apple: a Services ID as APPLE_CLIENT_ID, and in place of a fixed secret a key of the developer account - APPLE_TEAM_ID, APPLE_KEY_ID and the key itself in APPLE_PRIVATE_KEY (the PEM text of the .p8 file, or its path) - from which every token exchange makes the client secret Apple asks for: an ES256 JWT, signed with openssl.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;apple&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/bitbucket">oauth / bitbucket</a> <span class="about">Log in with Bitbucket (Bitbucket Cloud): an OAuth consumer of a workspace (Settings, OAuth consumers), its key and secret in BITBUCKET_CLIENT_ID and BITBUCKET_CLIENT_SECRET, its callback URL the address of the login page, and its permissions Account: Read and Email - a consumer&#039;s scopes are set there, not asked for in the address.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;bitbucket&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/discord">oauth / discord</a> <span class="about">Log in with Discord: an application of the developer portal, its id and secret in DISCORD_CLIENT_ID and DISCORD_CLIENT_SECRET.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;discord&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/forgejo">oauth / forgejo</a> <span class="about">Log in with Forgejo (or Gitea): Codeberg by default, or the forge at the address in the option base or FORGEJO_URL; an OAuth2 application&#039;s id and secret in FORGEJO_CLIENT_ID and FORGEJO_CLIENT_SECRET.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;forgejo&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/github">oauth / github</a> <span class="about">Log in with GitHub: an OAuth app (Settings, Developer settings), its client id and secret in GITHUB_CLIENT_ID and GITHUB_CLIENT_SECRET.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;github&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/gitlab">oauth / gitlab</a> <span class="about">Log in with GitLab: an application of gitlab.com, or of a GitLab of your own with its address in the option base or GITLAB_URL; its id and secret in GITLAB_CLIENT_ID and GITLAB_CLIENT_SECRET.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;gitlab&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/google">oauth / google</a> <span class="about">Sign in with Google, OpenID Connect: a client of the Google Cloud console, its id and secret in GOOGLE_CLIENT_ID and GOOGLE_CLIENT_SECRET.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;google&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/linkedin">oauth / linkedin</a> <span class="about">Sign In with LinkedIn using OpenID Connect: an app of the developer portal with that product added, its client id and secret in LINKEDIN_CLIENT_ID and LINKEDIN_CLIENT_SECRET.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;linkedin&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/mastodon">oauth / mastodon</a> <span class="about">Administrator-registered application on one configured Mastodon 4.3+ instance (PKCE S256).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;mastodon&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/microsoft">oauth / microsoft</a> <span class="about">Sign in with Microsoft, OpenID Connect on the identity platform: an app registration&#039;s id and secret in MICROSOFT_CLIENT_ID and MICROSOFT_CLIENT_SECRET, the tenant in the option tenant or MICROSOFT_TENANT - common (default: work, school and personal accounts), organizations, consumers, or a tenant&#039;s own id or domain.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;microsoft&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/oidc">oauth / oidc</a> <span class="about">Any OpenID Connect provider - Keycloak, Authentik, Zitadel, Auth0, Okta, a company&#039;s own: its issuer in the option issuer or OIDC_ISSUER, its client in OIDC_CLIENT_ID and OIDC_CLIENT_SECRET (or another prefix with the option env=&#039;KEYCLOAK&#039; - KEYCLOAK_ISSUER, KEYCLOAK_CLIENT_ID ...).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;oidc&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/slack">oauth / slack</a> <span class="about">Sign in with Slack, OpenID Connect: a Slack app with its redirect URL set under OAuth &amp; Permissions, its client id and secret in SLACK_CLIENT_ID and SLACK_CLIENT_SECRET.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;slack&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/oauth/x">oauth / x</a> <span class="about">Log in with X (Twitter): a Web App of the X developer console - a confidential client, with OAuth 2.0 switched on and the login page as its callback URL - its client id and secret in X_CLIENT_ID and X_CLIENT_SECRET.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">pad:oauth</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;x&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">href</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;#example&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="subsystem-channel">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Notification channels</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/database">channel / database</a> <span class="about">The notification kept on the site, for the person to read there: the {pad:notifications/} tag lists the unread ones of the user logged in, each with a link that marks it read; padNotifications and padNotificationRead do the same from PHP.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;database&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/discord">channel / discord</a> <span class="about">A message to a Discord channel through a webhook: its address in DISCORD_WEBHOOK_URL, or the option url - never a field of the recipient, as for slack.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;discord&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/googlechat">channel / googlechat</a> <span class="about">Google Chat incoming webhook delivery with mention-neutralized text.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;googlechat&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/gotify">channel / gotify</a> <span class="about">A message to a self-hosted Gotify server (gotify.net), which pushes it to the phones and browsers its user is signed in on: the server in GOTIFY_URL - never the recipient&#039;s - and an application&#039;s token in GOTIFY_TOKEN, sent as a bearer token in the Authorization header, which the server reads beside its own X-Gotify-Key: the header the reports redact by its name, and never the query&#039;s token=, which a proxy&#039;s log keeps.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;gotify&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/mail">channel / mail</a> <span class="about">The notification as an e-mail, through padMail and the transport it is set up with ($padMailTransport - a file under DATA/mail/ by default): the @mail@ section, or the plain text, is the HTML part, the text part is made from it, the title is the subject.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;mail&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/matrix">channel / matrix</a> <span class="about">A message in a Matrix room, sent by a bot account: the homeserver in MATRIX_HOMESERVER (https://matrix.org; never the recipient&#039;s), the account&#039;s access token in MATRIX_TOKEN, the room - its id, !abc:matrix.org, not an alias #name - in the recipient&#039;s field matrix or MATRIX_ROOM.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;matrix&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/mattermost">channel / mattermost</a> <span class="about">A message to a Mattermost channel through an incoming webhook: its address, https://&lt;server&gt;/hooks/&lt;id&gt;, in MATTERMOST_WEBHOOK_URL, or the option url - never a field of the recipient, as for slack; the id in it is its credential, which the reports redact.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;mattermost&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/ntfy">channel / ntfy</a> <span class="about">A push to the phones and desktops subscribed to an ntfy topic: the topic in the recipient&#039;s field ntfy or NTFY_TOPIC - letters, digits, _ and - -, the server in NTFY_URL (https://ntfy.sh unless given; never the recipient&#039;s), an access token in NTFY_TOKEN when the topic is protected.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;ntfy&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/pushover">channel / pushover</a> <span class="about">A push to the phones and desktops of a Pushover user, or of every member of a Pushover group, through its Message API (https://pushover.net/api): the application&#039;s token in PUSHOVER_TOKEN, the user&#039;s or group&#039;s key in the recipient&#039;s field pushover or PUSHOVER_USER - each 30 letters and digits.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;pushover&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/slack">channel / slack</a> <span class="about">A message to a Slack channel through an incoming webhook: its address in SLACK_WEBHOOK_URL, or the option url - never a field of the recipient: a webhook posts to the one channel it was made for, and its address is this server&#039;s to choose.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;slack&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/sms">channel / sms</a> <span class="about">A text message through an HTTP gateway of the generic shape: a POST to SMS_URL of JSON { &quot;to&quot;: ..., &quot;from&quot;: ..., &quot;text&quot;: ...</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;sms&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/teams">channel / teams</a> <span class="about">A message to a Microsoft Teams channel through a workflow&#039;s webhook (&quot;Post to a channel when a webhook request is received&quot;): its address in TEAMS_WEBHOOK_URL, or the option url - never a field of the recipient, as for slack.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;teams&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/telegram">channel / telegram</a> <span class="about">A message from a Telegram bot: the bot&#039;s token in TELEGRAM_BOT_TOKEN, the chat in the recipient&#039;s field telegram or TELEGRAM_CHAT_ID - its id, a number (negative for a group), or a channel&#039;s @name; a handle of a person is none, a bot cannot write to one.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;telegram&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/twilio">channel / twilio</a> <span class="about">A text message through Twilio&#039;s Messaging API (the Message resource of its REST API 2010-04-01): the account in TWILIO_ACCOUNT_SID - AC and 32 hex digits, in the address of the resource - and its auth token in TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN as the password of HTTP basic authentication, the account&#039;s SID the user; with an API key in TWILIO_API_KEY (SK and 32) the key is the user and TWILIO_AUTH_TOKEN holds its secret.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;twilio&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/webhook">channel / webhook</a> <span class="about">The notification as JSON posted to an address of the application&#039;s choosing - Zapier, n8n, a service of its own - and signed as Standard Webhooks has it (standardwebhooks.com): the address in WEBHOOK_URL, or the option url - never a field of the recipient - and the secret in WEBHOOK_SECRET, whsec_ and the base64 of 24 to 64 random bytes as the spec has a symmetric one (the prefix optional), or several split by spaces while a new one is rolled in.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;webhook&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/webpush">channel / webpush</a> <span class="about">A push message to the browsers the person allowed to notify them, also with the site closed: the recipient&#039;s field webpush holds the subscription the browser gave - PushSubscription.toJSON (), as an array or its JSON - or a list of them.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;webpush&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/channel/zulip">channel / zulip</a> <span class="about">Zulip stream or direct messages with mention-neutralized text and explicit recipients.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-fn">padNotify</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span> <span class="hl-var">$recipient</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order-shipped&#039;</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;order&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">=&gt;</span> <span class="hl-num">42</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span><span class="hl-pun">,</span> <span class="hl-pun">[</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;zulip&#039;</span> <span class="hl-pun">]</span> <span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span>
<span class="hl-var">$results</span> <span class="hl-pun">=</span> <span class="hl-fn">padNotifyResults</span> <span class="hl-pun">(</span><span class="hl-pun">)</span><span class="hl-pun">;</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="subsystem-filter">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Image filters</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/ascii">filter / ascii</a> <span class="about">The picture as text, a &lt;pre&gt; in place of the &lt;img&gt;: columns= characters across (64), as many lines as the picture&#039;s shape asks for, each character one of chars= - the lightest first (&#039; .:-=+*#%@&#039;) - by the darkness of the part it stands for.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;ascii&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/atkinson">filter / atkinson</a> <span class="about">Bill Atkinson&#039;s dither of the first Macintosh: like dither, but only three quarters of each pixel&#039;s error carried on, to six neighbours - lighter, higher contrast; levels= and colors= as dither&#039;s.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;atkinson&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/autocontrast">filter / autocontrast</a> <span class="about">The picture&#039;s tones stretched over the whole range: its darkest pixels made black and its lightest white, cutoff= percent (1) of them at each end let go first, so a grey, misty or faded picture gets its depth back - the colours keep their relations.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;autocontrast&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/bayer">filter / bayer</a> <span class="about">Ordered dithering: every pixel weighed against Bayer&#039;s threshold map of 8 by 8, the cross-hatch of an old screen; levels= and colors= as dither&#039;s.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;bayer&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/blur">filter / blur</a> <span class="about">The picture out of focus by radius= CSS pixels (6), the same on the 2x picture.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;blur&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/blurhash">filter / blurhash</a> <span class="about">A placeholder: Wolt&#039;s BlurHash of the picture in data-blurhash, and the hash decoded again as a small PNG behind the &lt;img&gt; - the blur of its colours the page shows until the picture has loaded.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;blurhash&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/colormatrix">filter / colormatrix</a> <span class="about">Transform normalized RGBA channels with four rows of five coefficients.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;colormatrix&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/dither">filter / dither</a> <span class="about">Floyd and Steinberg&#039;s error diffusion: the picture in levels= greys (2, ink and paper alone) from the first of colors= to the second, every pixel&#039;s error carried to its neighbours not yet drawn; made at 1x, its pixels sharp, written as a PNG.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;dither&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/dominant">filter / dominant</a> <span class="about">A placeholder: the picture&#039;s dominant colour behind the &lt;img&gt; while it loads, and in data-color for a page&#039;s own use.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;dominant&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/duotone">filter / duotone</a> <span class="about">The picture in two colours: its luminance laid on the line from the first of colors= in the shadows to the second in the highlights (&#039;#16213e, #f5c26b&#039;).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;duotone&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/edges">filter / edges</a> <span class="about">The outlines of the picture: Sobel&#039;s operator, how fast its brightness changes across and down every pixel, drawn from the first of colors= where nothing changes to the second where the change is strongest - white lines on black, or &#039;#fbfaf7, #1d1d1b&#039; for a drawing in ink on paper; amount= percent (100) makes the lines stronger or fainter.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;edges&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/emboss">filter / emboss</a> <span class="about">Reveal edges as a gray relief with a directional luminance kernel.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;emboss&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/gamma">filter / gamma</a> <span class="about">Adjust the midtones in encoded sRGB channel space.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;gamma&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/grayscale">filter / grayscale</a> <span class="about">The picture in shades of grey, each pixel its luminance: 0.299 of its red, 0.587 of its green and 0.114 of its blue, the weights of television (GD&#039;s grayscale filter).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;grayscale&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/halftone">filter / halftone</a> <span class="about">The picture as dots of ink on paper, an SVG in place of the &lt;img&gt;: a dot every cell= pixels (8) on rows set off by half a dot, its area the darkness there; the first of colors= the ink, the second the paper.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;halftone&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/invert">filter / invert</a> <span class="about">The negative of the picture, every channel 255 less itself (GD&#039;s negate filter).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;invert&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/lqip">filter / lqip</a> <span class="about">A placeholder: a low quality image of the picture, 16 pixels on its longer side and blurred, as a PNG in the page behind the &lt;img&gt; until the picture has loaded.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;lqip&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/pixelate">filter / pixelate</a> <span class="about">The picture in blocks of cell= pixels (8), each block the average of what it covers (GD&#039;s pixelate filter, its advanced form); made at 1x and shown with its pixels sharp, written as a PNG unless format= says otherwise.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;pixelate&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/posterize">filter / posterize</a> <span class="about">Every colour channel cut to levels= steps (4), as a screen print of flat areas; written as a PNG unless format= says otherwise.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;posterize&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/sepia">filter / sepia</a> <span class="about">The brown of an old photograph: every pixel through the classic sepia matrix, amount= percent of the way from the picture to it (100).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;sepia&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/sharpen">filter / sharpen</a> <span class="about">The edges of the picture crisper by amount= percent (50): every pixel minus the pixels around it, a convolution of GD&#039;s.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;sharpen&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/solarize">filter / solarize</a> <span class="about">The Sabattier effect of the darkroom, a print flashed with light while it develops: every channel at threshold= percent (50) of its full value or more turned into its negative, 255 less itself, the rest left as it was - the highlights go dark and strange, the shadows stay.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;solarize&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/threshold">filter / threshold</a> <span class="about">The picture in two colours alone, a hard cut: every pixel whose luminance is threshold= percent (50) of white or more the second of colors= (paper), every other one the first (ink) - a stencil, a stamp, a scanned page made clean, where dither spreads the error on to keep the greys.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;threshold&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/thumbhash">filter / thumbhash</a> <span class="about">A placeholder: Evan Wallace&#039;s ThumbHash of the picture in data-thumbhash, and the hash decoded again as a small PNG behind the &lt;img&gt; - more of the picture than a BlurHash in fewer bytes, its proportion and its transparency kept.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;thumbhash&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/filter/vignette">filter / vignette</a> <span class="about">The corners of the picture darker, as an old lens draws them, by amount= percent (50) at the very corner, the middle untouched.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">image:thumbnail</span> <span class="hl-str">&#039;photo.jpg&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">alt</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;A landscape&#039;</span>, <span class="hl-opt">width</span>=<span class="hl-num">320</span>, <span class="hl-opt">filter</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;vignette&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">/}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
  </div>
</section>

<section class="section" id="subsystem-formula">
  <div class="section-head"><h2>Spreadsheet functions</h2></div>
  <div class="cheat">
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/abs">formula / abs</a> <span class="about">ABS(number) is a number without its sign.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sheet:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">formulas</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;G1=ABS(-4.5)&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
Item,Qty,Price,Date,Flow
Apples,<span class="hl-num">10</span>,<span class="hl-num">1.20</span>,<span class="hl-num">2024</span>-<span class="hl-num">01</span>-<span class="hl-num">15</span>,-<span class="hl-num">70000</span>
Pears,<span class="hl-num">5</span>,<span class="hl-num">0.80</span>,<span class="hl-num">2024</span>-<span class="hl-num">02</span>-<span class="hl-num">29</span>,<span class="hl-num">12000</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/and">formula / and</a> <span class="about">AND(logical, ...) is TRUE when every value is: TRUE, a number that is not 0, the text TRUE; of a range its texts and empty cells are left out.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sheet:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">formulas</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;G1=AND(TRUE, 1, &quot;TRUE&quot;)&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/average">formula / average</a> <span class="about">AVERAGE(number, ...) is the mean of numbers, taken as SUM takes them: of a range only its numbers, so an empty cell does not count as 0.</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/averageif">formula / averageif</a> <span class="about">AVERAGEIF(range, criteria, [average_range]) is the mean of the cells of average_range - else of range - where range meets the criteria (see COUNTIF); without any #DIV/0!.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sheet:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">formulas</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;G1=AVERAGEIF(A2:A7, &quot;Apples&quot;, B2:B7)&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/averageifs">formula / averageifs</a> <span class="about">AVERAGEIFS(average_range, criteria_range, criteria, ...) is the mean of the cells of average_range where every criteria_range meets its criteria - pairs of a range and a criterion (see COUNTIF), each range the size and shape of average_range, else #VALUE!.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sheet:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">formulas</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;G1=AVERAGEIFS(B2:B7, A2:A7, &quot;Apples&quot;)&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

Pears,<span class="hl-num">5</span>,<span class="hl-num">0.80</span>,<span class="hl-num">2024</span>-<span class="hl-num">02</span>-<span class="hl-num">29</span>,<span class="hl-num">12000</span>

    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/ceiling">formula / ceiling</a> <span class="about">CEILING(number, significance) rounds a number up to a multiple of the significance: a positive number away from zero, a negative one toward zero with a positive significance and away from zero with a negative one.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sheet:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">formulas</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;G1=CEILING(2.5, 1)&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/choose">formula / choose</a> <span class="about">CHOOSE(index, value, ...) is the index-th of the values, from 1; an index below 1 or past the values is #VALUE!.</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/concat">formula / concat</a> <span class="about">CONCAT(text, ...) joins texts into one - every cell of a range in its order, a number as it reads in text, TRUE and FALSE in capitals, an empty cell as nothing.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sheet:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">formulas</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;G1=CONCAT(&quot;a&quot;, 1, TRUE)&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

<span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">/sheet:grid</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span></code></pre>
    </div>
    
    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/correl">formula / correl</a> <span class="about">CORREL(array1, array2) returns the Pearson correlation of paired numbers.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sheet:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">formulas</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;G1=CORREL(B2:B7, B2:B7)&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

Pears,<span class="hl-num">5</span>,<span class="hl-num">0.80</span>,<span class="hl-num">2024</span>-<span class="hl-num">02</span>-<span class="hl-num">29</span>,<span class="hl-num">12000</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/pmt">formula / pmt</a> <span class="about">PMT(rate, nper, pv, [fv], [type]) is the payment of each period of a loan or a saving - rate the interest of a period, nper the number of periods, pv what it is worth now, fv what it is worth at the end (0), type 1 to pay at the start of each period (0, at the end).</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sheet:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">formulas</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;G1=ROUND(PMT(8%/12, 10, 10000), 2)&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/sumproduct">formula / sumproduct</a> <span class="about">SUMPRODUCT(array, ...) multiplies the cells of ranges place by place and adds the products up: the ranges are walked in step, row by row, so they must have as many rows and columns as one another, else #VALUE!.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sheet:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">formulas</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;G1=SUMPRODUCT(B2:B7, C2:C7)&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>

    <div class="cheat-item">
      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/value">formula / value</a> <span class="about">VALUE(text) is the number a text writes - 1,250.50, 12%, $4, a date or a time; a text that writes none is #VALUE!.</span>

      <a class="name" href="?kind/formula/year">formula / year</a> <span class="about">YEAR(date) is the year of a date - a serial, or a text that reads as a date.</span>
      <pre class="code"><code><span class="hl-brc">{</span><span class="hl-tag">sheet:grid</span> <span class="hl-opt">formulas</span>=<span class="hl-str">&#039;G1=YEAR(D3)&#039;</span><span class="hl-brc">}</span>
